<script>
	
	
	

	console.log("Template:  == valentines-day");

	
			
					
						regionalRedirect();
					
					
	

	function regionalRedirect() {
		if (!isCookieStoreSet()) {
			setCookieStore();
		} else if(shouldRedirect()) {
			redirect();
		}
	}

	function isCookieStoreSet() {
		var cookieStore = Cookies.get("store_origin_v3");
		return typeof cookieStore === "string" && cookieStore.length > 0;
	}

	function setCookieStore() {
		jQuery.ajax( {
			url: "https://api.ipstack.com/check?access_key=706c5ff95b82d6cd7ce6d5879d6da764",
			type: "GET",
			success: onFetchedLocation
		} );
	}

	function saveRedirect(origin) {
		Cookies.set(
			"store_origin_v3",
			origin,
			{ 
				domain: "d1milano.com",
				expires: 90 
			}
		);
	}

	function onFetchedLocation(location) {
		if (isCountryISOCodeServed(location.country_code)) {
			saveRedirect("https://eu.d1milano.com/");
		} else {
			saveRedirect("https://d1milano.com/");
			redirect();
		}
	}

	function isCountryISOCodeServed(code) {
		var areListedCountriesExcluded = false;
		var listedCountriesISOCodesMap = getServedCountryISOCodesMap();
		var codeMatchesList = listedCountriesISOCodesMap[code.toUpperCase()] || false;
		return areListedCountriesExcluded ? !codeMatchesList : codeMatchesList;
	}

	function getServedCountryISOCodesMap() {
		
		var codes = {
			
			"AT": true,
			
			"BE": true,
			
			"CY": true,
			
			"EE": true,
			
			"FI": true,
			
			"FR": true,
			
			"DE": true,
			
			"GR": true,
			
			"IE": true,
			
			"IT": true,
			
			"LV": true,
			
			"LT": true,
			
			"LU": true,
			
			"MT": true,
			
			"NL": true,
			
			"PT": true,
			
			"SK": true,
			
			"SI": true,
			
			"ES": true,
			
			"CH": true,
			
			"GB": true,
			
		};
		return codes;
	}

	function redirect() {
		var storeOrigin = "https://d1milano.com/";
		var path = window.top.location.pathname;
		var newLocation = trimRightSlashes(storeOrigin) + "/" + trimLeftSlashes(path) + window.location.search;
		window.top.location = newLocation;
	}

	function shouldRedirect() {
		return !isCorrectRegion();
	}

	function isCorrectRegion() {
		var cookieStoreOrigin = Cookies.get("store_origin_v3");
		return (
			typeof cookieStoreOrigin === "string" &&
			sameHost(cookieStoreOrigin, "https://eu.d1milano.com/")
		);
	}


	function sameHost(urlA, urlB) {
		var originA = getHost(urlA);
		var originB = getHost(urlB);
		return originA !== null && originB !== null && originA === originB;
	}

	function getHost(url) {
		var matches = url.match(/https?:\/\/([^\/]+)/);
		return Array.isArray(matches) && matches.length > 1 ? matches[1] : null;
	}

	function trimLeftSlashes(str) {
		return str.replace(/^(\/+)/g, "");
	}

	function trimRightSlashes(str) {
		return str.replace(/(\/)+$/g, "");
	}
</script>

<script>
	/**
		* Copyright (c) 2017, inkOfPixel, Srl.
		* All rights reserved.
		*/

	if (window.addEventListener) {
	window.addEventListener("load", onLoad, false);
} else if (window.attachEvent) {
	element.attachEvent("onload", onLoad);
}

Transifex.live.onTranslatePage(updateCartFormLocale);

function onLoad() {
	var language_code =  Transifex.live.getSelectedLanguageCode();
	updateCartFormLocale(language_code);
}

function updateCartFormLocale(language_code) {
	if (window.location.pathname.match(/^\/cart/)) {
	var forms = document.getElementsByTagName("form");
	for (var i = 0; i < forms.length; i++) {
	if (forms[i].action.match(/cart/) && language_code) {
	forms[i].action = "/cart?locale=" + language_code;
			}
		}
	}
}

</script>

			$(function () {
				var searchBarToggle = $(".toggleSearch");
				var isSearchBarOpen = false;
				var searchContainer = $(".searchContainer");
				var searchInput = $(".search-input");


				searchBarToggle.click(function () {
					if (isSearchBarOpen === true) {
						searchContainer.removeClass( "active" );
						isSearchBarOpen = false;
					} else {
						searchContainer.addClass( "active" );
						isSearchBarOpen = true;
						setTimeout(function onTick() {
							searchInput.focus();
						}, 400);
					}

				});
			});

			/* Sometimes merchants change their shop currency, let's tell our JavaScript file */

			Currency.moneyFormats[Currency.shopCurrency].money_format = "€{{amount}}";

    $(function onReady() {
			/* Fix for customer account pages */
			jQuery('span.money span.money').each(function() {
				jQuery(this).parents('span.money').removeClass('money');
			});

			/* Saving the current price */

				jQuery(this).attr('data-currency-EUR', jQuery(this).html());
			});
    })

<script>

  var announcementBarHeight = $('.AnnouncementBar').outerHeight();
  var navigationBar = $('.NavigationBar');
  var navigationBarHeight = $('.NavigationBar').outerHeight();
  var mobileNavigation = $('.MobileNavigation');
  var mobileNavigationHeight = $('.MobileNavigation').outerHeight();
  var viewportHeight = window.innerHeight;

  if (announcementBarHeight == null){
    if (mobileNavigation.is(":visible")){
      $('.Hero').css({ height: viewportHeight - mobileNavigationHeight + "px" });
      $('.Hero .slide').css({ height: viewportHeight - mobileNavigationHeight + "px" });
    } else {
      $('.Hero').css({ height: viewportHeight - navigationBarHeight + "px" });
      $('.Hero .slide').css({ height: viewportHeight - navigationBarHeight + "px" });
    }
  } else {
    if (mobileNavigation.is(":visible")){
      $('.Hero').css({ height: viewportHeight - announcementBarHeight - mobileNavigationHeight + "px" });
      $('.Hero .slide').css({ height: viewportHeight - announcementBarHeight - mobileNavigationHeight + "px" });
    } else {
      $('.Hero').css({ height: viewportHeight - announcementBarHeight - navigationBarHeight + "px" });
      $('.Hero .slide').css({ height: viewportHeight - announcementBarHeight - navigationBarHeight + "px" });
    }
  }

</script>

<script>
	handleSelectedStoreChange();

	function handleSelectedStoreChange() {
		$("#StoreSelector .storeSelectorOption").click(function onClick() {
			if (this.id === "https://d1milano.com/") {
				Cookies.set(
					"store_origin_v3",
					"https://d1milano.com/",
					{ 
						domain: "d1milano.com",
						expires: 90 
					}
				);
				var path = window.location.pathname;
				window.location = trimRightSlashes("https://d1milano.com/") + "/" + trimLeftSlashes(path);
			}
		});
	}

	function trimLeftSlashes(str) {
		return str.replace(/^(\/+)/g, "");
	}

	function trimRightSlashes(str) {
		return str.replace(/(\/)+$/g, "");
	}
</script>

<script>
function trackElementClick(event) {
	var node = event.currentTarget;
	ga('send', 'event', {
		eventCategory: node.dataset.trackingCategory,
		eventAction: node.dataset.trackingAction,
		eventLabel: node.dataset.trackingLabel
	});
}

function reloadTrackingListeners() {
  var trackingNodeList = document.querySelectorAll("[data-tracking-category]");

  for (var i = 0 ; i < trackingNodeList.length; i++) {
    trackingNodeList[i].addEventListener('click' , trackElementClick) ; 
  }
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  reloadTrackingListeners();
});

window.reloadTrackingListeners = reloadTrackingListeners;
</script>
